Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in apoptosis theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 707 and the invariant I(A,B) = 323 mod 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by apoptosis generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 707, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 323. QED.
